1295: class Feeder:
1296:     """S2: owned writer of a frozen RAM proof buffer into ONE checker's stdin pipe; hashes/counts exactly the
1297:     bytes os.write() accepted.  Modes: plain; suffix (C3: SUFFIX after the proof); hold (C7b: the first write
1298:     waits until the checker was reaped).  The runner's read end is closed right after Popen (close_read);
1299:     the write end is closed exactly once: by the thread, or by abandon() when the thread never started."""
1300: 
1301:     def __init__(self, blocks, mode="plain", label=""):
1302:         if mode not in ("plain", "suffix", "hold"):
1303:             raise RuntimeError("unknown feed mode %r" % (mode,))
1304:         if not isinstance(blocks, tuple):
1305:             raise RuntimeError("proof buffer not frozen")
1306:         self.blocks, self.mode, self.label = blocks, mode, label
1307:         self.expected = sum(len(b) for b in blocks)
1308:         self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
1309:         self.fed = self.proof_fed = 0
1310:         self.delivery_error = self.exception = self.close_error = None
1311:         self.hold = threading.Event()
1312:         self.hold_released_by_event = None
1313:         self.started = False
1314:         self.finished = None
1315:         self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
1316:         self.rfd, self.wfd = os.pipe()             # non-inheritable: only the checker's dup2'd stdin holds rfd
1317:         LIVE_FEEDERS.append(self)                  # V5: charged while it holds the tuple
1318: 
1319:     def plan(self):
1320:         return {"mode": self.mode, "buffer_bytes": self.expected, "blocks": len(self.blocks)}
1321: 
1322:     def close_read(self):
1323:         fd, self.rfd = self.rfd, None
1324:         if fd is not None:
1325:             os.close(fd)
1326: 
1327:     def start(self):
1328:         self.thread.start()
1329:         self.started = True
1330: 
1331:     def abandon(self):
1332:         """Never-started feeder: close each still-open pipe end independently and drop the blocks.  V6: returns the
1333:         close errors so that the caller incorporates them before any settlement is published."""
1334:         errs = []
1335:         for name in ("rfd", "wfd"):
1336:             fd = getattr(self, name)
1337:             setattr(self, name, None)
1338:             if fd is not None:
1339:                 try:
1340:                     os.close(fd)
1341:                 except OSError as exc:
1342:                     errs.append("%s %r" % (name, exc))
1343:         self.blocks = ()
1344:         if errs:
1345:             self.close_error = "; ".join(errs)
1346:         return errs
1347: 
1348:     def _deliver(self, data):
1349:         mv, off = memoryview(data), 0
1350:         while off < len(mv):
1351:             try:
1352:                 n = os.write(self.wfd, mv[off:off + WRITE_SLICE])
1353:             except OSError as exc:
1354:                 self.delivery_error = "%s after %d fed bytes" % (
1355:                     "EPIPE" if isinstance(exc, BrokenPipeError) else repr(exc), self.fed)
1356:                 return off, False
1357:             self.sha.update(mv[off:off + n])
1358:             off += n
1359:             self.fed += n
1360:         return off, True
1361: 
1362:     def _run(self):
1363:         try:
1364:             if self.mode == "hold":
1365:                 self.hold_released_by_event = self.hold.wait(HOLD_SECONDS)
1366:             ok = True
1367:             for b in self.blocks:
1368:                 k, ok = self._deliver(b)
1369:                 self.proof_fed += k
1370:                 if not ok:
1371:                     break
1372:             if ok and self.mode == "suffix":
1373:                 self._deliver(SUFFIX)
1374:         except Exception as exc:
1375:             self.exception = repr(exc)
1376:         finally:
1377:             fd, self.wfd = self.wfd, None
1378:             if fd is not None:
1379:                 try:
1380:                     os.close(fd)                   # EOF for the checker
1381:                 except OSError as exc:
1382:                     self.close_error = repr(exc)
1383: 
1384:     def finish(self, timeout):
1385:         """After the checker is reaped: release a held feed, bounded join, record; drops the shared blocks."""
1386:         if self.finished is not None:
1387:             return self.finished
1388:         if self.started:
1389:             self.hold.set()
1390:             self.thread.join(timeout)
1391:         alive = self.started and self.thread.is_alive()
1392:         self.finished = {"mode": self.mode, "started": self.started, "buffer_bytes": self.expected,
1393:                          "fed_bytes": self.fed, "fed_sha256": self.sha.hexdigest(), "proof_bytes_fed": self.proof_fed,
1394:                          "undelivered_proof_bytes": self.expected - self.proof_fed,
1395:                          "delivery_error": self.delivery_error, "exception": self.exception,
1396:                          "close_error": self.close_error, "hold_released_by_event": self.hold_released_by_event,
1397:                          "thread_alive_after_join": alive}
1398:         if not alive:
1399:             self.blocks = ()
1400:         return self.finished

1875:     def _keep(self, b):
1876:         room = LOG_CAP - self.capture_written
1877:         if room > 0:
1878:             chunk = b[:room]
1879:             self.capture.write(chunk)
1880:             self.capture_written += len(chunk)
1881:         self.captured += len(b)
1882:         if self.captured > LOG_CAP:
1883:             self.over_cap = True
1884: 
1885:     def _read(self):
1886:         """Capped capture.  S3: for a checker child the raw stream is hashed/counted in-stream and ONLY lines that
1887:         fullmatch DUP_LINE at a genuine line start are dropped (counted by phase and bytes); V8: after an over-long
1888:         partial line is flushed, the rest of that physical line is kept unconditionally."""
1889:         f = self.filt
1890:         try:
1891:             while True:
1892:                 b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
1893:                 if not b:
1894:                     if f is not None:
1895:                         f["raw_eof"] = True
1896:                     break
1897:                 if f is None:
1898:                     self._keep(b)
1899:                     continue
1900:                 f["raw_sha"].update(b)
1901:                 f["raw_bytes"] += len(b)
1902:                 parts = (f["pending"] + b).split(b"\n")
1903:                 f["pending"] = parts.pop()
1904:                 for ln in parts:
1905:                     ln += b"\n"
1906:                     if f["cont"]:                  # V8: tail of a flushed physical line, never a whole line
1907:                         f["cont"] = False
1908:                         self._keep(ln)
1909:                         continue
1910:                     m = DUP_LINE.fullmatch(ln)
1911:                     if m is None:
1912:                         hm = PARSE_LINE.fullmatch(ln)
1913:                         if hm is not None and f["header_clauses"] is None:
1914:                             f["header_clauses"] = int(hm.group(2))
1915:                         self._keep(ln)
1916:                         continue
1917:                     f["dup_bytes"] += len(ln)
1918:                     if f["header_clauses"] is not None and int(m.group(3)) <= f["header_clauses"]:
1919:                         f["dup_low"] += 1
1920:                     else:
1921:                         f["dup_high"] += 1
1922:                 if len(f["pending"]) > PENDING_MAX:
1923:                     self._keep(f["pending"])
1924:                     f["pending"] = b""
1925:                     f["cont"] = True
1926:                     f["flushes"] += 1
1927:             if f is not None and f["pending"]:
1928:                 f["tail"] = len(f["pending"])
1929:                 self._keep(f["pending"])
1930:                 f["pending"] = b""
1931:         except Exception as exc:
1932:             self.reader_error = repr(exc)
1933:         finally:
1934:             for what, fn in (("flush", self.capture.flush), ("fsync", lambda: os.fsync(self.capture.fileno())),
1935:                              ("capture close", self.capture.close), ("stdout close", self.proc.stdout.close)):
1936:                 try:
1937:                     fn()                           # V6: every cleanup step independent
1938:                 except Exception as exc:
1939:                     self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " %s:%r" % (what, exc)
1940: 
1941:     def late_obligations(self):
1942:         """B1: paths and pipes an unreaped child can still touch after the runner returns (published with UNSETTLED)."""
1943:         obl = [{"path": self.cwd, "kind": "dir", "note": "the child's cwd and TMPDIR: may gain entries"},
1944:                {"path": self.out_path, "kind": "file", "note": "capture: written only by this runner's reader thread, "
1945:                                                                 "which ends when the runner returns"},
1946:                {"what": "stdout/stderr pipe", "note": "the runner's read end closes when it returns; EPIPE/SIGPIPE"}]
1947:         if self.feeder is not None:
1948:             obl.append({"what": "stdin pipe", "note": "the runner's write end closes when it returns (EOF)"})
1949:         if self.sink_obj is not None:
1950:             obl.append({"path": self.sink_obj.fifo_path, "kind": "fifo",
1951:                         "note": "proof FIFO: the child may still hold its write end"})
1952:             fp = getattr(self.sink_obj, "file_path", None)
1953:             if fp is not None:
1954:                 obl.append({"path": fp, "kind": "file", "note": "partial proof file: written only by this runner's "
1955:                                                                 "collector thread, which ends when the runner returns"})
1956:         return obl
1957: 
1958:     def alive(self):
1959:         return self.observe_exit() is None
1960: 
1961:     def census(self):
1962:         """V1: owned-process census through libproc (no /bin/ps): descendants of the child (own group + PPID
1963:         closure) and the resident bytes of the child plus every listed descendant; N12: None if ANY of them is
1964:         unreadable (never mapped to zero).  Raises on a failed listing (the monitor trips)."""
1965:         found = descendants(self.pid, self.pgid)
1966:         rss = task_rss(self.pid)
1967:         for d in found:
1968:             r = task_rss(d)
1969:             rss = None if (rss is None or r is None) else rss + r
1970:         return found, rss
1971: 
1972:     def timing(self):
1973:         """N4/N10: monotonic evidence relative to the run start, plus epoch exit/deadline values."""
1974:         rel = lambda t: None if t is None else round(t - STATE["mono0"], 4)   # noqa: E731
1975:         return {"popen_call_s": rel(self.popen_call_mono), "started_s": rel(self.started_mono),
1976:                 "last_alive_s": rel(self.last_alive_mono), "exit_observed_s": rel(self.exit_observed_mono),
1977:                 "deadline_s": rel(self.deadline_mono), "exit_observed_epoch": self.exit_observed_epoch,
1978:                 "deadline_epoch": self.deadline}
1979: 
1980:     def settle(self):
1981:         """Always reaches wait(); settlement final only after streams, transport endpoints, sink, cwd/run-tree
1982:         checks, hashes, N8 confinement and the receipt; every cleanup outcome is incorporated BEFORE the record is
1983:         published; N4: settled requires completion within the child's deadline + SETTLE_TAIL (monotonic and
1984:         epoch), and the record states whether the exit was observed by the deadline; any failure clears
1985:         'settled' and latches."""
1986:         if self.settled_rec is not None:
1987:             return self.settled_rec
1988:         problems = []
1989:         rc = self.reap_owned(TERM_WAIT)            # B1: bounded; None = survivor recorded UNSETTLED
1990:         if rc is None:
1991:             problems.append("not reaped within the bounded stop grace: UNSETTLED (reservation retained; parent "
1992:                             "disposition)")
1993:         if self.quarantined and self.quarantine_receipt_state != "written":
1994:             problems.append("UNSETTLED receipt not published (%s)" % self.quarantine_receipt_state)
1995:         if self.quarantined:
1996:             problems.append("UNSETTLED: chunk claim voided, parent disposition")
1997:         if self.term_refused_mono is not None:
1998:             problems.append("stop refused: identity unverifiable (UNSETTLED)")
1999:         stream_alive = None
2000:         if self.reader is not None and self.reader.ident is not None:
2001:             self.reader.join(STREAM_JOIN)
2002:             stream_alive = self.reader.is_alive()
2003:         else:
2004:             problems.append("no started capture reader")  # r6#2: explicit settlement failure
2005:             for what, fn in (("stdout close", self.proc.stdout.close), ("capture close", self.capture.close)):
2006:                 try:
2007:                     fn()                           # V6: independent closes
2008:                 except Exception as exc:
2009:                     problems.append("%s %r" % (what, exc))
2010:         if self.reader_error is not None:
2011:             problems.append("capture reader error %s" % self.reader_error)
2012:         feed_rec = filt_rec = None
2013:         if self.feeder is not None:
2014:             if not self.feeder.started:
2015:                 self.fd_errors += self.feeder.abandon()   # V6: before the record is published
2016:             try:
2017:                 feed_rec = self.feeder.finish(STREAM_JOIN)
2018:                 if not feed_rec["started"] or feed_rec["thread_alive_after_join"]:
2019:                     problems.append("stdin feeder not started or unsettled")
2020:                 if feed_rec["exception"] is not None or feed_rec["close_error"] is not None:
2021:                     problems.append("stdin feeder exception/close error")
2022:             except BaseException as exc:
2023:                 problems.append("feeder finish failed %r" % exc)
2024:             if stream_alive is False:
2025:                 f = self.filt
2026:                 filt_rec = {"raw_bytes": f["raw_bytes"], "raw_sha256": f["raw_sha"].hexdigest(), "raw_eof": f["raw_eof"],
2027:                             "header_clauses": f["header_clauses"], "dup_bytes_dropped": f["dup_bytes"],
2028:                             "dup_lines_at_or_below_header_clauses": f["dup_low"],
2029:                             "dup_lines_above_header_clauses": f["dup_high"], "nondropped_bytes": self.captured,
2030:                             "capture_file_bytes": self.capture_written, "unterminated_tail_bytes": f["tail"],
2031:                             "over_long_line_flushes": f["flushes"]}
2032:         if self.fd_errors:
2033:             problems.append("descriptor close errors %r" % self.fd_errors)
2034:         group_empty = None
2035:         try:
2036:             group_empty = list_pids(PROC_PGRP_ONLY, self.pgid) == []   # V1: zombies are listed too
2037:             if not group_empty:
2038:                 problems.append("process group not empty after reap")
2039:         except BaseException as exc:
2040:             problems.append("group census failed %r" % exc)
2041:         try:
2042:             after = descendants(self.pid, self.pgid)
2043:             if after:
2044:                 problems.append("descendants after reap %r" % after[:10])
2045:         except BaseException as exc:
2046:             problems.append("post-reap census failed %r" % exc)
2047:         if self.descendants:
2048:             problems.append("descendants seen during run %r" % self.descendants[:5])
2049:         sink_rec = None
2050:         if self.sink_obj is not None:
2051:             try:
2052:                 sink_rec = self.sink_obj.finish()
2053:                 if not sink_rec["sink_settled"]:
2054:                     problems.append("proof collector not settled")
2055:             except BaseException as exc:
2056:                 problems.append("collector finish failed %r" % exc)
2057:         try:
2058:             listing = os.listdir(TREE[self.cwd][0])   # N8: through the anchored cwd descriptor
2059:             if listing:
2060:                 problems.append("child cwd not empty: %r" % listing[:10])
2061:         except BaseException as exc:
2062:             problems.append("cwd check failed %r" % exc)
2063:         try:
2064:             expected = {self.out_path, self.launch_path, self.settle_path, self.quarantine_path}  # S1: no proof file
2065:             if self.sink_obj is not None:
2066:                 expected.add(self.sink_obj.fifo_path)     # N8: a written FIFO's times change
2067:             now = run_tree_census()
2068:             changed = sorted(p for p in set(now) | set(self.tree_before)
2069:                              if p not in expected and now.get(p) != self.tree_before.get(p))
2070:             if changed:
2071:                 problems.append("unexpected run-tree changes (added/changed/removed) %r" % changed[:10])
2072:         except BaseException as exc:
2073:             problems.append("run-tree census failed %r" % exc)
2074:         problems += confinement_refusals()                # N8
2075:         diag = []
2076:         out_sha = None
2077:         if stream_alive is False:
2078:             try:
2079:                 out_sha, out_len = sha_tree(self.out_path, limit=LOG_CAP)
2080:                 if out_len != self.capture_written:
2081:                     problems.append("capture file bytes %d != written %d" % (out_len, self.capture_written))
2082:                 else:
2083:                     register_written(self.out_path, out_sha, out_len)
2084:                 text = read_tree(self.out_path, LOG_CAP).decode("utf-8", "replace")
2085:                 diag = sorted(set(m.group(0) for m in RESOURCE_DIAG.finditer(text)))
2086:             except BaseException as exc:
2087:                 problems.append("capture hash/scan failed %r" % exc)
2088:         if diag:
2089:             latch("native memory diagnostic in %s: %r" % (self.label, diag))  # r3#3
2090:         exit_by_deadline = (self.exit_observed_mono is not None and self.exit_observed_mono <= self.deadline_mono
2091:                             and self.exit_observed_epoch <= self.deadline)
2092:         done_mono, done_epoch = time.monotonic(), time.time()       # N4: settlement completion (before publication)
2093:         if done_mono > self.deadline_mono + SETTLE_TAIL or done_epoch > self.deadline + SETTLE_TAIL:
2094:             problems.append("settlement completed after the child deadline + SETTLE_TAIL")
2095:         timing = dict(self.timing(), settle_done_s=round(done_mono - STATE["mono0"], 4),
2096:                       cleanup_s=None if self.exit_observed_mono is None else round(done_mono - self.exit_observed_mono, 4))
2097:         rec = {"seq": self.seq, "label": self.label, "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
2098:                "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start, "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc,

2102:                "exit_observed_by_deadline": exit_by_deadline,

2105:                "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
2106:                "stream_thread_alive_after_join": stream_alive, "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty,
2107:                "quarantined": self.quarantined, "problems": problems, "resource_diagnostics": diag,
2108:                "proof_sink": sink_rec, "stdin_feed": feed_rec, "stdout_filter": filt_rec,
2109:                "launch_record": self.launch_path, "output_path": self.out_path,

2112:               and self.reader_error is None and out_sha is not None)

2131: def supervise(child, sink):
2132:     """Monitor until exit or a single stop request; any monitor exception is itself a trip.  V1: census and RSS
2133:     through libproc; V3/N7: absolute AND elapsed (monotonic) deadlines; N4: last_alive_mono (taken BEFORE each
2134:     poll that saw the child alive) and the exit observation (taken AFTER the poll that saw the exit) bound the
2135:     exit time; V6: a capture reader error trips; N1: stops go through Child.term (one verified group SIGTERM)."""
2136:     me = os.getpid()
2137:     last_rss = last_reg = float("-inf")
2138:     peak = 0
2139:     while True:
2140:         mono = time.monotonic()
2141:         if child.observe_exit() is not None:
2142:             break
2143:         child.last_alive_mono = mono
2144:         try:
2145:             now = time.time()
2146:             if cancelled():
2147:                 child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
2148:             elif now > child.deadline or mono > child.deadline_mono:
2149:                 child.term("absolute_or_elapsed_deadline")
2150:             elif sink is not None and sink.over_cap:
2151:                 child.term("proof_cap")
2152:             elif sink is not None and sink.error:
2153:                 child.term("proof_sink_error")
2154:             elif child.feeder is not None and child.feeder.exception is not None:
2155:                 child.term("feeder_exception")
2156:             elif child.reader_error is not None:
2157:                 child.term("capture_reader_error")
2158:             elif child.over_cap:
2159:                 child.term("capture_cap")
2160:             elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
2161:                 child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
2162:             if not child.stop_requested() and mono - last_rss >= 1.0:
2163:                 last_rss = mono
2164:                 found, rss = child.census()
2165:                 runner_rss = task_rss(me)
2166:                 if found:
2167:                     child.descendants.extend(found)
2168:                     child.term("descendant_process")
2169:                 elif runner_rss is None or (rss is None and child.alive()):
2170:                     child.term("rss_unreadable")
2171:                 else:
2172:                     total = (rss or 0) + max(runner_rss, buffer_charge())  # S5/V5/N6: proof RAM charged in full
2173:                     peak = max(peak, total)
2174:                     if total > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
2175:                         child.term("lane_rss")
2176:             if not child.stop_requested() and mono - last_reg >= 5.0:
2177:                 last_reg = mono
2178:                 if registry_refusals():
2179:                     child.term("stop_registry")
2180:         except Exception as exc:
2181:             child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
2182:         if child.stop_requested():
2183:             break
2184:         time.sleep(SUPERVISE_SLICE)
2185:     if child.stop is not None and child.stop not in NONLATCHING_TRIPS:
2186:         latch("trip %s on %s" % (child.stop, child.label))
2187:     return child.stop, peak

2190: def run_child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink=None, feeder=None):
2191:     """Prepared outputs -> admission -> spawn -> supervise -> settle.  Returns (rec|None, lines, refusal)."""
2192:     ch = None
2193:     try:
2194:         cwd = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "cwd", "%02d-%s" % (STATE["seq"] + 1, label))
2195:         mkdir_anchored(cwd)  # r4#4: inside the cleanup guard; N8: exclusive and anchored
2196:         ch = Child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink, cwd, feeder)
2197:         if ch.refused:
2198:             return None, [], ch.refused
2199:         peak = None
2200:         try:
2201:             if cancelled():
2202:                 ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
2203:             _, peak = supervise(ch, sink)
2204:         except BaseException as exc:
2205:             latch("runner exception during supervision %r" % exc)
2206:             ch.term("runner_exception")
2207:         rec = ch.settle()
2208:         rec["trip"] = ch.stop
2209:         rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
2210:         lines = segments(ch.out_path) if rec.get("stream_thread_alive_after_join") is False else []
2211:         return rec, lines, []
2212:     finally:
2213:         settle_all()
2214:         if sink is not None and (ch is None or ch.proc is None):
2215:             sink.finish()  # never-spawned producer: unblock and join the reader
2216:         if feeder is not None and not feeder.started:
2217:             errs = feeder.abandon()  # never-spawned checker: both pipe ends closed; V6: errors latch
2218:             if errs:
2219:                 latch("never-started feeder endpoint close errors %r" % (errs,))

2367: def qualify_stream(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_cap, checks, cnf_post, buf_post):
2368:     """S4/V9/V10: item-level qualification over the solver leg (complete output exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']), the
2369:     RAM buffer and every pinned checker in order; checks = [(name, drat_rec, drat_lines), ...].  UNSAT requires []."""
2370:     bad = []
2371:     if solver_rec.get("returncode") != 20 or solver_rec.get("stop") != "self":
2372:         bad.append(defect("SOLVER_EXIT", "solver not self-exit rc20"))
2373:     if kissat_lines != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
2374:         bad.append(defect("SOLVER_OUTPUT", "complete solver output %r != ['s UNSATISFIABLE']" % (kissat_lines[:4],)))
2375:     buf = solver_rec.get("proof_sink") or {}
2376:     if not sink_complete(solver_rec.get("proof_sink")):
2377:         bad.append(defect("BUFFER_INCOMPLETE", "proof buffer incomplete/over cap/error"))
2378:     buf_sha, buf_bytes = buf.get("sha256_of_retained"), buf.get("retained")
2379:     if not isinstance(buf_bytes, int) or isinstance(buf_bytes, bool) or buf_bytes <= 0 or buf_bytes > proof_cap:
2380:         bad.append(defect("BUFFER_SIZE", "proof size outside (0, cap]"))
2381:     if [c[0] for c in checks] != [c[0] for c in CHECKERS]:
2382:         bad.append(defect("CHECKER_SET", "not every pinned checker ran, in order"))
2383:     for name, rec, lines in checks:
2384:         bad += checker_defects(name, cnf_expect, buf_sha, buf_bytes, rec, lines)
2385:     if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
2386:         bad.append(defect("CNF_POST", "CNF file digest/bytes != expected"))
2387:     if buf_post != [buf_sha, buf_bytes]:
2388:         bad.append(defect("BUFFER_POST", "proof buffer re-hash after the checks != collector measurement"))
2389:     if not solver_rec.get("settled") or solver_rec.get("capture_over_cap") is not False:
2390:         bad.append(defect("SOLVER_SETTLE", "settlement/capture defect seq %s" % solver_rec.get("seq")))
2391:     if solver_rec.get("exit_observed_by_deadline") is not True:
2392:         bad.append(defect("SOLVER_TIMING", "exit not observed by the solver deadline (N4)"))
2393:     return bad

3036: def final_code(summary, outcome):
3037:     """r3#9/N9/B1: blocking closeout metadata first: host clock (owned helper), child and helper SETTLEMENT (N2: a
3038:     helper counts only if its settlement predicate held), proof RAM (N6: releases are retried, anything still held
3039:     is a defect), N8 anchored confinement, run-tree bytes and digest preservation of every runner-written file, N4
3040:     closeout timing, B1 the UNSETTLED owners (exact identities, live census, late obligations) with the reservation
3041:     retained until a NEW parent disposition; then the explicit closeout boundary; the cancel stamp is read AFTER the
3042:     boundary and compared with it (N9).  Any closeout defect latches (exit 50); exit 0 is impossible with a voided
3043:     chunk claim.  Nothing here waits for an owner."""
3044:     out_dir = STATE["out"]
3045:     host_end = clock()                             # V1: owned helper; None after a cancel, a latch or a failure
3046:     children = [c.settled_rec if c.settled_rec is not None else {"seq": c.seq, "label": c.label, "pid": c.pid,
3047:                                                                    "settled": False, "note": "never settled"}
3048:                 for c in OWNED if c.proc is not None]
3049:     all_settled = all(r.get("settled") for r in children)
3050:     helpers_settled = all(h.settled for h in HELPERS)
3051:     unsettled_owners = [dict(c.unsettled, kind="child", seq=c.seq, label=c.label, receipt=c.quarantine_path,
3052:                              receipt_state=c.quarantine_receipt_state)
3053:                         for c in OWNED if c.proc is not None and c.unsettled is not None] + \
3054:                        [dict(h.unsettled, kind="helper", launch_order=h.launch_order, receipt=h.quarantine_path,
3055:                              receipt_state=h.quarantine_receipt_state)
3056:                         for h in HELPERS if h.unsettled is not None]
3057:     for b in list(LIVE_BUFFERS):
3058:         if b.release_requested and not b.released:
3059:             b.release()                            # N6: retried now that collectors may have ended
3060:     try:
3061:         outstanding = buffer_charge()
3062:         buffers = [b.accounting() for b in LIVE_BUFFERS][:LIST_MAX]
3063:     except BaseException as exc:
3064:         outstanding, buffers = None, ["accounting failed %r" % (exc,)]
3065:     confinement = confinement_refusals()
3066:     try:
3067:         tree = run_tree_census()
3068:         tree_bytes = sum(v[3] for v in tree.values() if v[0] != "dir")
3069:         quarantine = sorted(p for p in tree if p.endswith(".QUARANTINE.json"))
3070:     except BaseException as exc:
3071:         tree_bytes, quarantine = None, ["run-tree census failed %r" % (exc,)]
3072:     preservation = preservation_refusals()
3073:     m, e = time.monotonic(), time.time()
3074:     elapsed = m - STATE["mono0"]
3075:     late = m > STATE["mono_end"] + SETTLE or e > STATE["abs_end"] + SETTLE
3076:     boundary = next(ORDER)
3077:     co = STATE["cancel_order"]                     # N9: sampled AFTER the closeout boundary
3078:     cancel_before = co is not None and co < boundary
3079:     launch_after_cancel = [c.seq for c in OWNED if c.proc is not None and co is not None and c.launch_order is not None
3080:                            and co < c.launch_order] + \
3081:                           ["helper %s" % h.launch_order for h in HELPERS if h.proc is not None and co is not None
3082:                            and co < h.launch_order]
3083:     closeout_defects = []
3084:     if not helpers_settled:
3085:         closeout_defects.append("metadata helper unsettled or quarantined")
3086:     if unsettled_owners:
3087:         closeout_defects.append("UNSETTLED owners %r: reservation retained; parent disposition required"
3088:                                 % ([u.get("pid") for u in unsettled_owners][:LIST_MAX],))
3089:     if outstanding != 0:
3090:         closeout_defects.append("proof RAM still held at closeout: %r" % (outstanding,))
3091:     closeout_defects += confinement
3092:     closeout_defects += preservation
3093:     if tree_bytes is None or tree_bytes > STATE["output_limit"]:
3094:         closeout_defects.append("run-tree bytes %r unreadable or above the reservation %d" % (tree_bytes,
3095:                                                                                              STATE["output_limit"]))
3096:     if quarantine:
3097:         closeout_defects.append("quarantine receipts present %r" % (quarantine[:4],))
3098:     if late:
3099:         closeout_defects.append("closeout after the frozen cutoffs + SETTLE (N4)")
3100:     if launch_after_cancel:
3101:         closeout_defects.append("launch ordered after a cancel: %r" % launch_after_cancel)
3102:     chunk_claim = "held" if (all_settled and helpers_settled and not quarantine and not unsettled_owners and not late
3103:                              and elapsed + SETTLE <= MAX_CHUNK) else "voided"
3104:     if chunk_claim == "voided":
3105:         closeout_defects.append("chunk claim voided")
3106:     for w in closeout_defects:
3107:         latch("closeout: " + w)
3108:     if outcome["sat"]:
3109:         code = EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE
3110:     elif LATCH or not all_settled:
3111:         code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
3112:     elif outcome["exception"]:
3113:         code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
3114:     elif cancel_before:
3115:         code = EXIT_CANCELLED
3116:     elif outcome["controls_failed"]:
3117:         code = EXIT_CONTROLS_FAILED
3118:     elif outcome["refused"]:
3119:         code = EXIT_ADMISSION_REFUSED
3120:     elif outcome["complete"]:
3121:         code = EXIT_PLAN_COMPLETE
3122:     else:
3123:         code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
3124:     summary.update(exit_code=code, latch=list(LATCH), cancel_order=co, closeout_boundary=boundary,
3125:                    cancel_before_closeout=cancel_before, host_clock_end=host_end,
3126:                    children=[{k: r.get(k) for k in ("seq", "label", "pid", "returncode", "stop", "settled",
3127:                                                     "launch_order", "exit_observed_by_deadline")} for r in children],
3128:                    all_children_settled=all_settled, metadata_helpers=HELPER_LOG[:LIST_MAX],
3129:                    unsettled_owners=unsettled_owners[:LIST_MAX], reservation_retained=bool(unsettled_owners),
3130:                    release_requires=("a NEW parent disposition confirming that every listed process ended"
3131:                                      if unsettled_owners else "verified parent release"),
3132:                    all_helpers_settled=helpers_settled, proof_ram_outstanding_bytes=outstanding,
3133:                    proof_buffers=buffers, quarantine_receipts=quarantine, run_tree_bytes=tree_bytes,
3134:                    retained_bytes_charged=STATE["written"], output_limit_bytes=STATE["output_limit"],
3135:                    elapsed_mono_s=round(elapsed, 3), closeout_late=late, chunk_claim=chunk_claim,
3136:                    closeout_defects=closeout_defects, lane_ledger_last=STATE["last_ledger"])
3137:     try:
3138:         write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
3139:     except BaseException as exc:
3140:         print("SUMMARY WRITE FAILED %r" % exc, flush=True)
3141:         code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
3142:     print("SUMMARY", os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), "exit", code, flush=True)
3143:     return code

### test_pilot_stream_v5.py
